+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+
+async function main() {
+const base = process.argv[2]!;
+const { instanceKey, runSurface } = await import(`${base}/engine.ts`);
+const { closureProblems } = await import(`${base}/defineSurface.ts`);
+const { normalize } = await import(`${base}/normalizer.ts`);
+const { templateFormat } = await import(`${base}/templateFormat.ts`);
+const { parseDocument } = await import("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js");
+
+const ROWS = ["r"];
+const raw = (tag: string) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: ROWS });
+const text = (tag: string, over: any = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path}: type", ...over });
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: map", unknownMessage: "{path}: unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const open = (tag: string, entry: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: open", keyLaneAt: "container", entry, ...over,
+});
+const list = (tag: string, member: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "list", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: list", memberLaneAt: "index", member, ...over,
+});
+const surface = (root: any, extra: any = {}) => ({ substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (s: any, value: any, catalog: any = { resolve: () => null }) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+function print(label: string, value: unknown) { console.log(label, JSON.stringify(value)); }
+
+// Site 1: original case, both orders; then memberOf, unique, map members,
+// nested open-map entries and dependsOn tags at member + enclosing depths.
+const member = text("idm", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad" }, gating: true });
+const listRoot = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm"] } }),
+}));
+const a1 = [
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["taken", "BAD"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { TAKEN: "1" }, ids: ["TAKEN"] }).findings,
+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }],
+]);
+print("A1_LIST", a1);
+
+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

A3_ARRAY [{"path":"a","message":"a: non-string"},{"path":"arr[0]","message":"arr[0]: non-string"},{"path":"arr[1]","message":"arr[1]: non-string"}]
B_REPEAT_PER_KEY [{"path":"a","message":"a: non-string"},{"path":"a","message":"a: non-string"}]
D_LIVE_CLOSURE []

+++ b//tmp/round9-probe.ts

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+
+async function main() {
+const base = process.argv[2]!;
+const { instanceKey, runSurface } = await import(`${base}/engine.ts`);
+const { closureProblems } = await import(`${base}/defineSurface.ts`);
+const { normalize } = await import(`${base}/normalizer.ts`);
+const { templateFormat } = await import(`${base}/templateFormat.ts`);
+const { parseDocument } = await import("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js");
+
+const ROWS = ["r"];
+const raw = (tag: string) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: ROWS });
+const text = (tag: string, over: any = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path}: type", ...over });
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: map", unknownMessage: "{path}: unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const open = (tag: string, entry: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: open", keyLaneAt: "container", entry, ...over,
+});
+const list = (tag: string, member: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "list", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: list", memberLaneAt: "index", member, ...over,
+});
+const surface = (root: any, extra: any = {}) => ({ substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (s: any, value: any, catalog: any = { resolve: () => null }) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+function print(label: string, value: unknown) { console.log(label, JSON.stringify(value)); }
+
+// Site 1: original case, both orders; then memberOf, unique, map members,
+// nested open-map entries and dependsOn tags at member + enclosing depths.
+const member = text("idm", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad" }, gating: true });
+const listRoot = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm"] } }),
+}));
+const a1 = [
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["taken", "BAD"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { TAKEN: "1" }, ids: ["TAKEN"] }).findings,
+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }],
+]);
+print("A1_LIST", a1);
+
+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));

+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);

+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+
+async function main() {
+const base = process.argv[2]!;
+const engine = await import(`${base}/engine.ts`);
+const { runSurface } = engine;
+const instanceKey = engine.instanceKey ?? ((segments: readonly (string | number)[], lane?: string) => JSON.stringify([segments, lane ?? null]));
+const { closureProblems } = await import(`${base}/defineSurface.ts`);
+const { normalize } = await import(`${base}/normalizer.ts`);
+const { templateFormat } = await import(`${base}/templateFormat.ts`);
+const { parseDocument } = await import("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js");
+const legacy = process.argv[3] === "legacy";
+const check = legacy ? { deepEqual() {}, equal() {}, notEqual() {} } : assert;
+
+const ROWS = ["r"];
+const raw = (tag: string) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: ROWS });
+const text = (tag: string, over: any = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path}: type", ...over });
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: map", unknownMessage: "{path}: unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const open = (tag: string, entry: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: open", keyLaneAt: "container", entry, ...over,
+});
+const list = (tag: string, member: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "list", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: list", memberLaneAt: "index", member, ...over,
+});
+const surface = (root: any, extra: any = {}) => ({ substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (s: any, value: any, catalog: any = { resolve: () => null }) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+function print(label: string, value: unknown) { console.log(label, JSON.stringify(value)); }
+
+// Site 1: original case, both orders; then memberOf, unique, map members,
+// nested open-map entries and dependsOn tags at member + enclosing depths.
+const member = text("idm", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad" }, gating: true });
+const listRoot = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm"] } }),
+}));
+const a1 = [
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["taken", "BAD"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { TAKEN: "1" }, ids: ["TAKEN"] }).findings,
+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }],
+]);
+print("A1_LIST", a1);
+
+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],

+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

codex
A célzott próbák mindhárom javítást igazolták, és ugyanaz a probe az előző commit byte-jain mindhárom hibát újra előállította: a listafinding eltűnt, az effective map egy elemre omlott, az alias csak az első címen jelent meg. Most a négy kért regressziós parancsot futtatom sorban; nincs párhuzamos tesztfolyamat.

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+
+async function main() {
+const base = process.argv[2]!;
+const engine = await import(`${base}/engine.ts`);
+const { runSurface } = engine;
+const instanceKey = engine.instanceKey ?? ((segments: readonly (string | number)[], lane?: string) => JSON.stringify([segments, lane ?? null]));
+const { closureProblems } = await import(`${base}/defineSurface.ts`);
+const { normalize } = await import(`${base}/normalizer.ts`);
+const { templateFormat } = await import(`${base}/templateFormat.ts`);
+const { parseDocument } = await import("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js");
+const legacy = process.argv[3] === "legacy";
+const check = legacy ? { deepEqual() {}, equal() {}, notEqual() {} } : assert;
+
+const ROWS = ["r"];
+const raw = (tag: string) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: ROWS });
+const text = (tag: string, over: any = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path}: type", ...over });
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: map", unknownMessage: "{path}: unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const open = (tag: string, entry: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: open", keyLaneAt: "container", entry, ...over,
+});
+const list = (tag: string, member: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "list", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: list", memberLaneAt: "index", member, ...over,
+});
+const surface = (root: any, extra: any = {}) => ({ substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (s: any, value: any, catalog: any = { resolve: () => null }) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+function print(label: string, value: unknown) { console.log(label, JSON.stringify(value)); }
+
+// Site 1: original case, both orders; then memberOf, unique, map members,
+// nested open-map entries and dependsOn tags at member + enclosing depths.
+const member = text("idm", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad" }, gating: true });
+const listRoot = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm"] } }),
+}));
+const a1 = [
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["taken", "BAD"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { TAKEN: "1" }, ids: ["TAKEN"] }).findings,
+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }],
+]);
+print("A1_LIST", a1);
+
+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+

+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);

+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);

+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {

+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+
+async function main() {
+const base = process.argv[2]!;
+const engine = await import(`${base}/engine.ts`);
+const { runSurface } = engine;
+const instanceKey = engine.instanceKey ?? ((segments: readonly (string | number)[], lane?: string) => JSON.stringify([segments, lane ?? null]));
+const { closureProblems } = await import(`${base}/defineSurface.ts`);
+const { normalize } = await import(`${base}/normalizer.ts`);
+const { templateFormat } = await import(`${base}/templateFormat.ts`);
+const { parseDocument } = await import("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/v3/node_modules/yaml/dist/index.js");
+const legacy = process.argv[3] === "legacy";
+const check = legacy ? { deepEqual() {}, equal() {}, notEqual() {} } : assert;
+
+const ROWS = ["r"];
+const raw = (tag: string) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: ROWS });
+const text = (tag: string, over: any = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path}: type", ...over });
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: map", unknownMessage: "{path}: unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const open = (tag: string, entry: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: open", keyLaneAt: "container", entry, ...over,
+});
+const list = (tag: string, member: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "list", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: list", memberLaneAt: "index", member, ...over,
+});
+const surface = (root: any, extra: any = {}) => ({ substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (s: any, value: any, catalog: any = { resolve: () => null }) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+function print(label: string, value: unknown) { console.log(label, JSON.stringify(value)); }
+
+// Site 1: original case, both orders; then memberOf, unique, map members,
+// nested open-map entries and dependsOn tags at member + enclosing depths.
+const member = text("idm", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad" }, gating: true });
+const listRoot = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm"] } }),
+}));
+const a1 = [
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["taken", "BAD"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { TAKEN: "1" }, ids: ["TAKEN"] }).findings,
+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }],
+]);
+print("A1_LIST", a1);
+
+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const ROWS = ["r"];
+const raw = (tag: string) => ({ kind: "raw", tag, rows: ROWS });
+const text = (tag: string, over: any = {}) => ({ kind: "string", tag, rows: ROWS, typeMessage: "{path}: type", ...over });
+const fixed = (tag: string, fields: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.fixed", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: map", unknownMessage: "{path}: unknown", fields, ...over,
+});
+const open = (tag: string, entry: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "map.open", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: open", keyLaneAt: "container", entry, ...over,
+});
+const list = (tag: string, member: any, over: any = {}) => ({
+  kind: "list", tag, rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "{path}: list", memberLaneAt: "index", member, ...over,
+});
+const surface = (root: any, extra: any = {}) => ({ substrate: templateFormat.substrate, root, valueClasses: {}, crossRules: [], normalizers: [], ...extra });
+const direct = (s: any, value: any, catalog: any = { resolve: () => null }) =>
+  runSurface(s, value, { channel: { kind: "direct" }, catalog });
+
+function print(label: string, value: unknown) { console.log(label, JSON.stringify(value)); }
+
+// Site 1: original case, both orders; then memberOf, unique, map members,
+// nested open-map entries and dependsOn tags at member + enclosing depths.
+const member = text("idm", { grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "{path}: bad" }, gating: true });
+const listRoot = surface(fixed("root", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm"] } }),
+}));
+const a1 = [
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["taken", "BAD"] }).findings,
+  direct(listRoot, { names: { TAKEN: "1" }, ids: ["TAKEN"] }).findings,
+];
+assert.deepEqual(a1, [
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }, { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: bad" }, { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: collision" }],
+  [{ path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" }],
+]);
+print("A1_LIST", a1);
+
+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

+const memberOfRoot = surface(fixed("r", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, {
+    memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.names" }, message: "{path}: missing", dependsOn: ["idm"] },
+    unique: { grain: "perOccurrence", at: "index", message: "{path}: duplicate" },
+  }),
+}));
+const memberOfOut = direct(memberOfRoot, { names: { ok: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "ghost", "ghost"] }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(memberOfOut, [
+  { path: "ids[0]", message: "ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "ids[1]", message: "ids[1]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: missing" },
+  { path: "ids[2]", message: "ids[2]: duplicate" },
+]);
+print("A1_MEMBEROF_UNIQUE", memberOfOut);
+
+const mapMembers = surface(list("maps", fixed("m", { id: member })));
+const mapOut = direct(mapMembers, [{ id: "BAD" }, { id: "good" }]).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(mapOut, [{ path: "$[0].id", message: "$[0].id: bad" }]);
+print("A1_MAP_MEMBERS", mapOut);
+
+const group = fixed("group", {
+  names: open("names", text("nv")),
+  ids: list("ids", member, { disjointFrom: { relation: "disjointFrom", target: { keysOf: "$.groups.*.names" }, message: "{path}: collision", dependsOn: ["idm", "names"] } }),
+});
+const nestedRoot = surface(fixed("root", { groups: open("groups", group) }));
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(nestedRoot), []);
+const nestedOut = direct(nestedRoot, { groups: { one: { names: { taken: "1" }, ids: ["BAD", "taken"] }, two: { names: {}, ids: [] } } }).findings;
+assert.deepEqual(nestedOut, [
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[0]", message: "groups.one.ids[0]: bad" },
+  { path: "groups.one.ids[1]", message: "groups.one.ids[1]: collision" },
+]);
+print("A1_NESTED_MULTI_DEPTH", nestedOut);
+
+// Site 2 writer: exact rendered collision ["a.b"] vs ["a","b"].
+const binding = (tag: string) => fixed(tag, {
+  uses: text(`${tag}u`),
+  config: { kind: "delegate", tag: `${tag}c`, rows: ROWS, registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses", beltMessage: "belt" },
+});
+const writerSurface = surface(fixed("r", { "a.b": binding("d"), a: fixed("a", { b: binding("n") }) }));
+const catalog = { resolve: () => ({ requiresRuntimeContext: false, validateAndNormalizeConfig: (v: any) => ({ ok: true, effective: { from: v.from } }) }) };
+const writer = direct(writerSurface, { "a.b": { uses: "x", config: { from: "dotted" } }, a: { b: { uses: "y", config: { from: "nested" } } } }, catalog);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.findings, []);
+assert.equal(writer.effectiveConfigs.size, 2);
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a.b"])), { from: "dotted" });
+assert.deepEqual(writer.effectiveConfigs.get(instanceKey(["a", "b"])), { from: "nested" });
+print("A2_WRITER", [...writer.effectiveConfigs]);
+
+// Site 2 reader: wildcard pipeline keys containing encoding punctuation,
+// numeric-looking strings, and dots all retain their structural segments.
+const pipeSurface = surface(fixed("r", { pipes: open("pipes", list("pl", binding("pb"))) }), {
+  normalizers: [{ tag: "norm", rows: ROWS, hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs", over: "$.pipes", carry: ["uses"], into: "config" }],
+});
+assert.deepEqual(closureProblems(pipeSurface), []);
+const weird = `x\"],null]`;
+const pipeValue: any = { pipes: { [weird]: [{ uses: "x" }], "0": [{ uses: "y" }], "a.b": [{ uses: "z" }] } };
+const pipeEff = new Map([
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", weird, 0]), { from: "weird" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), { from: "numeric-string" }],
+  [instanceKey(["pipes", "a.b", 0]), { from: "dot" }],
+]);
+normalize(pipeSurface, pipeValue, pipeEff);
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes[weird][0].config, { from: "weird" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["0"][0].config, { from: "numeric-string" });
+assert.deepEqual(pipeValue.pipes["a.b"][0].config, { from: "dot" });
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]));
+assert.notEqual(instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"]));
+print("A2_READER", pipeValue);
+print("A2_KEYS", [instanceKey(["pipes", "0", 0]), instanceKey(["pipes", 0, 0]), instanceKey(["a.b"]), instanceKey(["a", "b"])]);
+
+// Site 3: aliases across addresses and cycles. The scan is intentionally
+// on the root open map so nested arrays/maps are all in its subtree.
+const scanSurface = surface(open("g", raw("v"), { deepKeyStringness: { message: "{path}: non-string", channel: "file" } }));
+function file(yaml: string) {
+  const doc = parseDocument(yaml);
+  return runSurface(scanSurface, doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true }), { channel: { kind: "file", doc, source: yaml }, catalog: { resolve: () => null } }).findings;
+}
+const triple = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nb: *bad\nc: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(triple.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+print("A3_TRIPLE", triple);
+const cycleAliases = file("a: &loop\n  1: x\n  self: *loop\nb: *loop\n");
+assert.deepEqual(cycleAliases.filter((f: any) => f.message.endsWith("non-string")).map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "b"]);
+print("A3_ALIAS_CYCLE", cycleAliases);
+const depths = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\nnested:\n  z: *bad\n");
+assert.deepEqual(depths.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "nested.z"]);
+print("A3_DEPTHS", depths);
+const arrays = file("a: &bad\n  1: x\narr: [*bad, *bad]\n");
+assert.deepEqual(arrays.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "arr[0]", "arr[1]"]);
+print("A3_ARRAY", arrays);
+const twoBadKeys = file("a:\n  1: x\n  2: y\n");
+assert.deepEqual(twoBadKeys.map((f: any) => f.path), ["a", "a"]);
+print("B_REPEAT_PER_KEY", twoBadKeys);
+
+print("D_LIVE_CLOSURE", closureProblems(templateFormat));
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });
